cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(nbody_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(nbody nbody.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(nbody PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(nbody m)
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

    int numBodies = 1024;

        } else {

            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {

        }
    }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
+// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
+// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;
+
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
+                       const double* z, size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
+        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
+        px[k] = x[i];
+        py[k] = y[i];
+        pz[k] = z[i];
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            const double dx = xj - px[k];
+            const double dy = yj - py[k];
+            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
+        bodies[first + k].vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+        bodies[first + k].vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+        bodies[first + k].vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+    }

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    std::vector<double> x(n), y(n), z(n);
+    Body* const data = bodies.data();
+    double* const px = x.data();
+    double* const py = y.data();
+    double* const pz = z.data();
+
+    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
+    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, px, py, pz, n, numSteps)
+    {
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
+                computeForceBlock(data, px, py, pz, first, n);
+            }
+            // The force-loop barrier keeps the snapshot immutable until every
+            // thread has finished reading it. Refresh it during integration.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+                px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+                py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+                pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+            }
+            // The integration barrier publishes all positions for the next step.
+        }
     }
 }

+            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+                px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+                py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+                pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+            }
+            // The integration barrier publishes all positions for the next step.
+        }
     }
 }

+// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
+// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;
+
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
+                       const double* z, size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
+        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
+        px[k] = x[i];
+        py[k] = y[i];
+        pz[k] = z[i];
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            const double dx = xj - px[k];
+            const double dy = yj - py[k];
+            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
+        bodies[first + k].vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+        bodies[first + k].vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+        bodies[first + k].vel.z += DT * Fz[k];

+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    std::vector<double> x(n), y(n), z(n);
+    Body* const data = bodies.data();
+    double* const px = x.data();
+    double* const py = y.data();
+    double* const pz = z.data();
+
+    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
+    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, px, py, pz, n, numSteps)
+    {
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
+                computeForceBlock(data, px, py, pz, first, n);
+            }
+            // The force-loop barrier keeps the snapshot immutable until every
+            // thread has finished reading it. Refresh it during integration.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+                px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+                py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+                pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+            }
+            // The integration barrier publishes all positions for the next step.
+        }
     }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
-        
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
+// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
+// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;
+
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
+                       const double* z, size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
+        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
+        px[k] = x[i];
+        py[k] = y[i];
+        pz[k] = z[i];
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            const double dx = xj - px[k];
+            const double dy = yj - py[k];
+            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    std::vector<double> x(n), y(n), z(n);
+    Body* const data = bodies.data();
+    double* const px = x.data();
+    double* const py = y.data();
+    double* const pz = z.data();
+
+    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
+    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, px, py, pz, n, numSteps)
+    {
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
+                computeForceBlock(data, px, py, pz, first, n);
+            }
+            // The force-loop barrier keeps the snapshot immutable until every
+            // thread has finished reading it. Refresh it during integration.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+                px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+                py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+                pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+            }
+            // The integration barrier publishes all positions for the next step.
+        }
     }
 }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
-        
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
+// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
+// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;
+
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
+                       const double* z, size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
+        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
+        px[k] = x[i];
+        py[k] = y[i];
+        pz[k] = z[i];
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            const double dx = xj - px[k];
+            const double dy = yj - py[k];
+            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
+        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // kernel; contracting these updates would change subsequent timesteps.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[first + k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[first + k].vel.y += dvy;
+        bodies[first + k].vel.z += dvz;
+    }
 }

 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#ifndef _OPENMP
#error "This benchmark requires OpenMP support"
#endif

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;

void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
                       const double* z, size_t first, size_t n) {
    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};

    #pragma omp simd
    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
        px[k] = x[i];
        py[k] = y[i];
        pz[k] = z[i];
    }

    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
            const double dx = xj - px[k];
            const double dy = yj - py[k];
            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;

            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
        }
    }

    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
        // kernel; contracting these updates would change subsequent timesteps.
        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
        bodies[first + k].vel.x += dvx;
        bodies[first + k].vel.y += dvy;
        bodies[first + k].vel.z += dvz;
    }
}

void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    std::vector<double> x(n), y(n), z(n);
    Body* const data = bodies.data();
    double* const px = x.data();
    double* const py = y.data();
    double* const pz = z.data();

    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, px, py, pz, n, numSteps)
    {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
        }

        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
                computeForceBlock(data, px, py, pz, first, n);
            }
            // The force-loop barrier keeps the snapshot immutable until every
            // thread has finished reading it. Refresh it during integration.
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
                px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
                py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
                pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
            }
            // The integration barrier publishes all positions for the next step.
        }
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;

-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
+// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
+// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;
+
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
+                       const double* z, size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
+        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
+        px[k] = x[i];
+        py[k] = y[i];
+        pz[k] = z[i];
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            const double dx = xj - px[k];
+            const double dy = yj - py[k];
+            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
+        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // kernel; contracting these updates would change subsequent timesteps.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[first + k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[first + k].vel.y += dvy;
+        bodies[first + k].vel.z += dvz;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    std::vector<double> x(n), y(n), z(n);
+    Body* const data = bodies.data();
+    double* const px = x.data();
+    double* const py = y.data();
+    double* const pz = z.data();
+
+    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
+    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, px, py, pz, n, numSteps)
+    {
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
+                computeForceBlock(data, px, py, pz, first, n);
+            }
+            // The force-loop barrier keeps the snapshot immutable until every
+            // thread has finished reading it. Refresh it during integration.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
+                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
+                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
+                px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+                py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+                pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+            }
+            // The integration barrier publishes all positions for the next step.
+        }
     }
 }

+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {

+    std::vector<double> x(n), y(n), z(n);
+    Body* const data = bodies.data();

+    double* const pz = z.data();
+
+    // Small systems need enough blocks per worker to amortize team startup and
+    // barriers. Respect the OpenMP thread limit while avoiding excessive teams.
+    const int numThreads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),

codex
Using all 256 hardware threads added substantial overhead on small inputs. I’ve capped the team size according to the available work, while respecting OpenMP’s configured thread limit. I’m also combining force calculation and integration using separate position buffers to reduce synchronization to one barrier per step.

/bin/bash -lc 'cat nbody/nbody.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/nbody_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r1
 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#ifndef _OPENMP
#error "This benchmark requires OpenMP support"
#endif

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;

void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
                       const double* z, size_t first, size_t n) {
    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};

    #pragma omp simd
    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
        px[k] = x[i];
        py[k] = y[i];
        pz[k] = z[i];
    }

    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
            const double dx = xj - px[k];
            const double dy = yj - py[k];
            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;

            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
        }
    }

    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
        // kernel; contracting these updates would change subsequent timesteps.
        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
        bodies[first + k].vel.x += dvx;
        bodies[first + k].vel.y += dvy;
        bodies[first + k].vel.z += dvz;
    }
}

void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    std::vector<double> x(n), y(n), z(n);
    Body* const data = bodies.data();
    double* const px = x.data();
    double* const py = y.data();
    double* const pz = z.data();

    // Small systems need enough blocks per worker to amortize team startup and
    // barriers. Respect the OpenMP thread limit while avoiding excessive teams.
    const int numThreads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
        std::max(size_t{1}, n / (16 * FORCE_BLOCK))));

    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(data, px, py, pz, n, numSteps)
    {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
        }

        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
                computeForceBlock(data, px, py, pz, first, n);
            }
            // The force-loop barrier keeps the snapshot immutable until every
            // thread has finished reading it. Refresh it during integration.
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                data[i].pos.x += data[i].vel.x * DT;
                data[i].pos.y += data[i].vel.y * DT;
                data[i].pos.z += data[i].vel.z * DT;
                px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
                py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
                pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
            }
            // The integration barrier publishes all positions for the next step.
        }
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    simulateBodies(bodies, numSteps);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();

-        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;

-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
+// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
+// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;
+
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
+                      const double* z, double* nextX, double* nextY,
+                      double* nextZ, size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
+        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
+        px[k] = x[i];
+        py[k] = y[i];
+        pz[k] = z[i];
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            const double dx = xj - px[k];
+            const double dy = yj - py[k];
+            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
+        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // kernel; contracting these updates would change subsequent timesteps.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[first + k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[first + k].vel.y += dvy;
+        bodies[first + k].vel.z += dvz;
+
+        // All force reads use the old snapshot, so each target can be integrated
+        // immediately and publish its next position into a separate buffer.
+        bodies[first + k].pos.x += bodies[first + k].vel.x * DT;
+        bodies[first + k].pos.y += bodies[first + k].vel.y * DT;
+        bodies[first + k].pos.z += bodies[first + k].vel.z * DT;
+        nextX[first + k] = bodies[first + k].pos.x;
+        nextY[first + k] = bodies[first + k].pos.y;
+        nextZ[first + k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    // Double-buffer positions so integration cannot overwrite a position that
+    // another thread is still using in this timestep's force calculation.
+    std::vector<double> x(2 * n), y(2 * n), z(2 * n);
+    Body* const data = bodies.data();
+    double* const bufferX = x.data();
+    double* const bufferY = y.data();
+    double* const bufferZ = z.data();
+
+    // Small systems need enough blocks per worker to amortize team startup and
+    // barriers. Respect the OpenMP thread limit while avoiding excessive teams.
+    const int numThreads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+        std::max(size_t{1}, n / (16 * FORCE_BLOCK))));
+
+    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
+    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(data, bufferX, bufferY, bufferZ, n, numSteps)
+    {
+        double* px = bufferX;
+        double* py = bufferY;
+        double* pz = bufferZ;
+        double* nextX = bufferX + n;
+        double* nextY = bufferY + n;
+        double* nextZ = bufferZ + n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
+                advanceBodyBlock(data, px, py, pz, nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+            }
+            // The loop's barrier publishes the complete next snapshot and ends
+            // all old-snapshot reads before that buffer can be reused. These
+            // pointer swaps are private to each thread and need no extra barrier.
+            std::swap(px, nextX);
+            std::swap(py, nextY);
+            std::swap(pz, nextZ);
+        }
+    }
 }

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+// Vectorize independent target bodies, keeping the original source-body order
+// within each force sum. A position snapshot avoids reading velocity cache lines.
+constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;
+
+void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
+                      const double* z, double* nextX, double* nextY,
+                      double* nextZ, size_t first, size_t n) {
+    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
+        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
+        px[k] = x[i];
+        py[k] = y[i];
+        pz[k] = z[i];
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            const double dx = xj - px[k];
+            const double dy = yj - py[k];
+            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
+        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // kernel; contracting these updates would change subsequent timesteps.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[first + k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[first + k].vel.y += dvy;
+        bodies[first + k].vel.z += dvz;
+
+        // All force reads use the old snapshot, so each target can be integrated
+        // immediately and publish its next position into a separate buffer.
+        bodies[first + k].pos.x += bodies[first + k].vel.x * DT;
+        bodies[first + k].pos.y += bodies[first + k].vel.y * DT;
+        bodies[first + k].pos.z += bodies[first + k].vel.z * DT;
+        nextX[first + k] = bodies[first + k].pos.x;
+        nextY[first + k] = bodies[first + k].pos.y;
+        nextZ[first + k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
+    const size_t n = bodies.size();
+    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    // Double-buffer positions so integration cannot overwrite a position that
+    // another thread is still using in this timestep's force calculation.
+    std::vector<double> x(2 * n), y(2 * n), z(2 * n);
+    Body* const data = bodies.data();
+    double* const bufferX = x.data();
+    double* const bufferY = y.data();
+    double* const bufferZ = z.data();
+
+    // Small systems need enough blocks per worker to amortize team startup and
+    // barriers. Respect the OpenMP thread limit while avoiding excessive teams.
+    const int numThreads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+        std::max(size_t{1}, n / (16 * FORCE_BLOCK))));
+
+    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
+    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(data, bufferX, bufferY, bufferZ, n, numSteps)
+    {
+        double* px = bufferX;
+        double* py = bufferY;
+        double* pz = bufferZ;
+        double* nextX = bufferX + n;
+        double* nextY = bufferY + n;
+        double* nextZ = bufferZ + n;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
+            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
+            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
+                advanceBodyBlock(data, px, py, pz, nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
+            }
+            // The loop's barrier publishes the complete next snapshot and ends
+            // all old-snapshot reads before that buffer can be reused. These
+            // pointer swaps are private to each thread and need no extra barrier.
+            std::swap(px, nextX);
+            std::swap(py, nextY);
+            std::swap(pz, nextZ);
+        }
+    }
 }

    30	void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    31	    for (auto& body : bodies) {
    32	        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    33	        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    34	        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    35	        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    36	        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    37	        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    38	    }
    39	}

    45	void advanceBodyBlock(Body* bodies, const double* x, const double* y,
    46	                      const double* z, double* nextX, double* nextY,
    47	                      double* nextZ, size_t first, size_t n) {
    48	    alignas(64) double px[FORCE_BLOCK], py[FORCE_BLOCK], pz[FORCE_BLOCK];
    49	    alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
    50	
    51	    #pragma omp simd
    52	    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
    53	        // Pad the final block with an existing body; only real lanes are stored.
    54	        const size_t i = std::min(first + k, n - 1);
    55	        px[k] = x[i];
    56	        py[k] = y[i];
    57	        pz[k] = z[i];
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
    61	        const double xj = x[j], yj = y[j], zj = z[j];
    62	        #pragma omp simd
    63	        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
    64	            const double dx = xj - px[k];
    65	            const double dy = yj - py[k];
    66	            const double dz = zj - pz[k];
    67	            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
    68	            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
    69	            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
    70	
    71	            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
    72	            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
    73	            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
    74	        }
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    for (size_t k = 0; k < std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first); ++k) {
    78	        // Preserve the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
    79	        // kernel; contracting these updates would change subsequent timesteps.
    80	        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
    81	        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
    82	        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
    83	        bodies[first + k].vel.x += dvx;
    84	        bodies[first + k].vel.y += dvy;
    85	        bodies[first + k].vel.z += dvz;
    86

    94	        nextZ[first + k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;
    95	    }
    96	}
    97	
    98	void simulateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int numSteps) {
    99	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
   100	    if (n == 0 || numSteps <= 0) {
   101	        return;
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    // Double-buffer positions so integration cannot overwrite a position that
   105	    // another thread is still using in this timestep's force calculation.
   106	    std::vector<double> x(2 * n), y(2 * n), z(2 * n);
   107	    Body* const data = bodies.data();
   108	    double* const bufferX = x.data();
   109	    double* const bufferY = y.data();
   110	    double* const bufferZ = z.data();
   111	
   112	    // Small systems need enough blocks per worker to amortize team startup and
   113	    // barriers. Respect the OpenMP thread limit while avoiding excessive teams.
   114	    const int numThreads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
   115	        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
   116	        std::max(size_t{1}, n / (16 * FORCE_BLOCK))));
   117	
   118	    // The team persists across timesteps; static scheduling assigns equally
   119	    // expensive force blocks without scheduling overhead or shared reductions.
   120	    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(data, bufferX, bufferY, bufferZ, n, numSteps)
   121	    {
   122	        double* px = bufferX;
   123	        double* py = bufferY;
   124	        double* pz = bufferZ;
   125	        double* nextX = bufferX + n;
   126	        double* nextY = bufferY + n;
   127	        double* nextZ = bufferZ + n;
   128	
   129	        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
   130	        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   131	            px[i] = data[i].pos.x;
   132	            py[i] = data[i].pos.y;
   133	            pz[i] = data[i].pos.z;
   134	        }
   135	
   136	        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
   137	            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
   138	            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += FORCE_BLOCK) {
   139	                advanceBodyBlock(data, px, py, pz, nextX, nextY, nextZ, first, n);
   140	            }
   141	            // The loop's barrier publishes the complete next snapshot and ends
   142	            // all old-snapshot reads before that buffer can be reused. These
   143	            // pointer swaps are private to each thread and need no extra barrier.
   144	            std::swap(px, nextX);
   145	            std::swap(py, nextY);
   146	            std::swap(pz, nextZ);
   147	        }
   148	    }
   149	}
   150	
   151	double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   152	    double energy = 0.0;
   153	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
   154	    
   155	    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
   156	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   157	        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
   158	                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
   159	                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
   160	    }
   161	    
   162	    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
   163	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   164	        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
   165	            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
   166	            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
   167	            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
   168	            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
   169	            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
   170	        }
   171	    }
   172	    
   173	    return energy;
   174	}
